TL;DR: 本文提出了一套完整的基于强化学习的导航训练与部署流程，系统分析了影响sim-to-real迁移的关键因素（传感器噪声、感知失败、系统延迟、控制响应），并提出了扰动感知微调和基于Transformer的时间推理策略，在多种机器人平台上实现了零样本的仿真到现实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的自主导航研究大多聚焦于框架设计（如输入表示、动作空间、奖励函数），但对sim-to-real迁移的分析有限，缺乏关于训练策略如何影响真实部署性能的系统性研究。

Method: 1) 系统实证研究：解耦分析影响sim-to-real迁移的关键因素（传感器噪声、感知失败、系统延迟、控制响应）；2) 扰动感知微调：针对经验识别的领域差异进行后训练自适应；3) 基于Transformer的时间推理策略：利用短时域观测提升控制平滑性并缓解感知退化。

Result: 在静态和动态环境中均优于基于学习的基线方法，在静态场景下性能可媲美基于优化的规划器，并在无人机和足式机器人等平台上实现了零样本的仿真到现实迁移。

Conclusion: 通过系统分析sim-to-real迁移的关键因素并设计相应的训练策略与网络架构，可以显著提升基于强化学习的导航策略在真实世界中的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed significant progress in autonomous navigation using reinforcement learning. However, existing approaches largely emphasize reinforcement learning framework design, such as input representations, action spaces, and reward functions, while providing limited analysis of sim-to-real transfer and insufficient insight into how training strategies affect real-world deployment performance. To bridge this gap, we not only introduce an effective RL framework but also present a complete training and deployment pipeline, along with a systematic empirical study that disentangles the key factors affecting sim-to-real transfer in reinforcement learning-based navigation, including sensor noise, perception failures, system latency, and control response. Building on insights from this analysis, we introduce perturbation-aware fine-tuning, a post-training adaptation strategy that improves transfer robustness by explicitly accounting for empirically identified domain discrepancies. To further mitigate perception degradation and enhance control smoothness in real-world deployment, we propose a Transformer-based temporal reasoning policy that leverages short-horizon observation for navigation control. We quantitatively evaluate how individual sim-to-real perturbations and training design choices impact navigation performance across environments. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed training strategy and policy architecture outperform learning-based baselines in both static and dynamic environments, while achieving performance comparable to optimization-based planners in static settings. We validate our approach through real-world deployment on multiple robotic platforms, including aerial and legged robots, across navigation-centric tasks such as exploration and inspection, demonstrating zero-shot sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [112] [Wind-Aware Optimal Trajectory Planning for Efficient Gliding of Fixed-Wing Aerial Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15619)
*Luca Morando,Nishanth Bobbili,Giuseppe Loianno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Bernstein多项式的非线性多成本轨迹规划器，用于小型固定翼无人机滑翔飞行，通过差分平坦性映射控制指令，结合模拟净变距仪优化能量平衡状态，并在实际飞行中验证了其抗风扰和避障能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统总能量控制系统（TECS）在滑翔飞行中反应式调节动能与势能，需要精细调参和配平条件知识，缺乏规划层面的能量管理能力。

Method: 采用Bernstein多项式生成C^3连续轨迹，通过差分平坦性映射到控制指令；集成模拟净变距仪估计空气运动，约束滑翔至能量平衡状态；结合Dubins路径初始化巡航段，实现混合作战。

Result: 在CFD仿真和真实飞行实验中，该方法在风扰和障碍物条件下可靠地稳定了下沉率、空速和滑翔比。

Conclusion: 将能量调控从控制层提升到规划层是有效的，能够实现更鲁棒的滑翔能量管理，适用于混合动力任务场景。

Abstract: Gliding offers small fixed-wing UAVs extended endurance and silent operation but requires accurate energy management, especially under wind disturbances and obstacle constraints. Traditional Total Energy Control Systems based controllers regulate the trade between potential and kinetic energy reactively, often requiring fine-tuning and trim-conditions knowledge. In this work, we shift the regulation to the planning level and present a nonlinear, multi-cost trajectory planner for small UAV gliders. The method generates $\mathcal{C}^3$ continuous trajectories based on Bernstein polynomials, mapped into control commands through differential flatness, and re-planned online to match experimentally derived sink polar curves. A simulated netto variometer is integrated into the optimization to estimate air mass motion, constraining the glide to energy-balanced states. Consecutive gliding trajectories are linked by cruising segments computed through trajectories initialized on Dubins path-based waypoints, enabling hybrid missions that combine powered and unpowered flight. The approach is validated in CFD simulations and real-world experiments with a fixed-wing platform, showing reliable stabilization of sink rate, airspeed, and glide ratio under wind gusts and in presence of obstacles.

</details>


### [113] [Propagating Unsafe Actions in LLM Controlled Multi-Robot Collaboration via Single Robot Compromise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15641)
*Zhen Huang,Zhihuang Liu,Weishang Wu,Zhiping Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对多机器人系统的攻击范式（InfectBot），攻击者仅需操控一个入口机器人，通过机器人间通信传播恶意指令，导致整个系统产生不安全的协同行为。实验表明该攻击具有高服从性（1.00）、强传播性（0.90）和隐蔽性（0.81），仅需3轮即可感染所有机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注单机器人场景下LLM规划器的安全威胁，而多机器人协作场景中通过机器人间通信传播的安全风险尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出一种针对多机器人LLM规划器的攻击范式，攻击者仅与单个入口机器人交互，被攻陷的机器人通过同层通信传播恶意意图，使整个系统执行协同的不安全动作。

Result: 在失职、隐私泄露和公共安全三个高风险维度上，攻击表现出高服从性（最高1.00）、强感染性（0.90）和高效性（仅需3轮感染全部机器人），同时保持0.81的隐蔽性。

Conclusion: 多机器人LLM规划器存在持续的安全对齐漏洞，尤其是在处理紧急情况或权利冲突时，协调机制可能无意中允许对抗性指令覆盖安全要求，亟需加强安全对齐设计。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as general planners in embodied intelligence, enabling high level coordination and low level task planning for both single robot and multi-robot collaboration. This increasing reliance on embodied LLM planners also raises critical security concerns, since misaligned or manipulated instructions can be translated into physical actions. Prior work has studied such threats in single robot settings, while security risks in LLM controlled multi-robot collaboration, especially those propagated through inter robot communication, remain largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we propose a novel attack paradigm for multi-robot system in which the adversary interacts with only a single entry robot. The compromised robot then propagates malicious intent through peer communication, leading to coordinated unsafe actions across the system. Our evaluation, covering high risk dimensions of dereliction of duty, privacy compromise, and public safety hazards, reveals a persistent safety alignment gap in multi-robot planners. We quantify this process with three metrics, obedience, infectiousness, and stealthiness. Experiments demonstrate both persistent attacker control and rapid propagation: obedience reaches 1.00 in the strongest cases, and infectiousness rises to 0.90. Notably, the attack is highly efficient, requiring as few as 3.0 rounds to compromise all the robots while maintaining a stealthiness score of 0.81. Such risks are amplified when robots must resolve trade offs in critical situations, such as emergencies or conflicts of rights, because the coordination mechanism can unintentionally allow adversarial instructions to override safety requirements. The code is available at https://github.com/TheFatInsect/InfectBot.

</details>


### [114] [MyoChallenge 2025: A New Benchmark for Human Athletic Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15650)
*Cheryl Wang,Chun Kwang Tan,Balint K. Hodossy,Eric Lyu,Jun Guo,Wentao Zhao,Huaping Liu,Chengkun Li,Merkourios Simos,Bianca Ziliotto,Alexander Mathis,Siyuan Liu,Jiahao Chen,Shanlin Zhong,Bo Jiang,Ci Song,Yaoye Zhu,Chenhui Zuo,Yanan Sui,Mohamed Irfan Refai,Massimo Sartori,Guillaume Durandau,Vikash Kumar,Vittorio Caggiano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MyoChallenge 2025 (NeurIPS)提出了基于高保真肌肉骨骼模型的运动控制基准测试，包含乒乓球和足球罚球两个任务，吸引了近70支队伍和560+份投稿，促进了机器学习、生物力学、运动科学和神经科学的交叉研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI和机器人系统难以复现人类运动智能中快速决策与精确肌肉协调的结合，同时由于物理设备限制，生物运动中的复杂肌肉协调机制难以在体内测量，存在理解上的根本性空白。

Method: 构建高保真肌肉骨骼模型与物理仿真环境，设定上/下肢控制两条赛道（乒乓球上肢+躯干、足球罚球下肢+躯干），在MyoSuite开源框架中集成标准化任务与生理学真实模型，吸引跨学科团队提交算法。

Result: 征集到近70支团队、超过560份投稿；发展了多种SOTA肌肉骨骼运动控制算法，包括基于物理的运动规划器、同策略行为克隆、层级规划、肌肉协同等。

Conclusion: MyoChallenge'25作为可复现、可复用的测试平台，加速了机器学习、生物力学、运动科学和神经科学领域的交叉研究，弥合了对运动控制智能的理解鸿沟。

Abstract: Athletic performance represents the pinnacle of human motor intelligence, demanding rapid choices, precise control, agility, and coordinated physical execution. Replicating this seamless combination of capabilities remains elusive in current artificial intelligence and robotic systems. Concurrently, understanding the biological mastery of these movements is hindered because complex muscle coordination is rarely measured in vivo due to the limitations of physical equipment. To bridge this fundamental gap in understanding, MyoChallenge at NeurIPS 2025 established a pioneering benchmark for motor control intelligence in sports, leveraging high-fidelity musculoskeletal models within physics simulation combined with machine learning-driven algorithms. The competition introduces two distinct tracks emphasizing either upper or lower limbs control: a table tennis rally task utilizing a biomechanic upper limb composed of an arm with a hand and a trunk; and a soccer penalty kick using a biomechanic model of legs and a trunk. Marking the fourth iteration of the MyoChallenge series, this event attracted almost 70 teams and over 560 submissions globally, uniting a diverse community ranging from physicians and neuroscientists to machine learning experts. The competition facilitated the development of several state-of-the-art control algorithms for a musculoskeletal system capable of sports agility, leveraging techniques such as physics-based motion planners, on-policy behaviour cloning, hierarchical planning, and muscle synergies. By integrating standardized tasks and physiologically realistic models into the open-source framework of MyoSuite, MyoChallenge'25 serves as a reproducible and reusable testbed to accelerate interdisciplinary research across machine learning, biomechanics, sports science, and neuroscience. Project page: https://www.myosuite.org//myochallenge/myochallenge-2025.

</details>


### [115] [PCASim: Promptable Closed-loop Adversarial Simulation for Urban Traffic Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15654)
*Chuancheng Zhang,Zhenhao Wang,Kaizheng Li,Yaran Lin,Qiang Guo,Bin Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个结合对抗性场景生成与安全智能体训练的闭环测试框架，利用大语言模型整合知识、数据和对抗驱动方法，生成安全关键交通场景，并通过强化学习训练不同车辆行为，提升场景多样性和真实性。实验表明，领域特定语言生成准确率提升12%，场景转换成功率提升8%，避障能力提升30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究很少将对抗性场景生成与安全智能体训练在闭环测试中协同进化，缺乏两者相互促进的机制，难以高效测试自动驾驶在复杂城市环境中的鲁棒性。

Method: 构建对抗性行为知识库（基于规则过滤开源数据集+知识检索模块），利用大语言模型整合知识驱动、数据驱动和对抗驱动方法生成安全关键场景，并使用强化学习模型训练不同类型车辆的行为以丰富场景多样性。

Result: 领域特定语言生成准确率提升12%，新场景转换成功率提升8%，避障能力提升30%。

Conclusion: 所提框架能有效实现对抗性场景生成与智能体训练的协同进化，显著提升自动驾驶系统的安全测试能力。

Abstract: Real-world autonomous driving, particularly in urban environments with numerous corner cases, requires rigorous testing to ensure product safety and robustness. However, few studies have explored integrating adversarial scenario generation with the training of safety agents in closed-loop testing, enabling efficient co-evolution and mutual enhancement of both. To address this challenge, an adversarial behavior knowledge repository is constructed by applying rule-based filtering to an open-source dataset, combined with knowledge retrieval modules tailored for simulation environments. A large language model (LLM) is employed to integrate knowledge-, data-, and adversarial-driven approaches, generating safety-critical traffic scenarios customized to user needs. Additionally, while evaluating the generated scenarios, we employ reinforcement learning models to train the behaviors of different types of vehicles, thereby enriching scenario diversity beyond existing datasets while preserving realism. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed framework improves the accuracy of domain-specific language generation by 12\%. Moreover, the success rate of newly generated scenario transformations increases by 8\%, while obstacle-avoidance capability is enhanced by 30\%. For the complete manuscript, please refer to: https://zhenhaooo.github.io/PCASim.github.io/

</details>


### [116] [Feedback World Model Enables Precise Guidance of Diffusion Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15705)
*Tuo An,Jindou Jia,Gen Li,Jingliang Li,Chuhao Zhou,Pengfei Liu,Bofan Lyu,Jiaqi Bai,Xinying Guo,Geng Li,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出反馈世界模型(Feedback World Model)，通过在线更新轻量级反馈状态来闭环修正预测误差，在不额外训练的情况下显著提升世界模型在分布外场景的预测准确率和策略成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统世界模型在部署时遇到训练分布外的状态时预测不可靠，而执行过程中机器人能直接观测真实状态这一信号未被充分利用。作者希望利用这一实时观测信号来闭环修正预测误差。

Method: 提出反馈世界模型范式：在推理时维护一个轻量级反馈状态，通过在线更新来逐次修正未来预测。该方法可解释为潜在空间观测器，在一定条件下具有收敛保证。同时引入动作感知引导(Action-aware Guidance)，强调动作可控成分以提升控制效果。

Result: 在LIBERO-Plus、Robomimic和真实世界操作任务上，世界模型预测误差降低高达76.4%，分布外任务成功率提升30%。

Conclusion: 推理时引入实时反馈是一种简单而强大的替代静态世界建模的方案，能够有效应对分布偏移问题。

Abstract: World models aim to improve robotic decision making by predicting the consequences of actions. However, in practice, their predictions often become unreliable once the robot encounters states outside the training distribution, limiting their effectiveness at deployment. We observe that execution itself provides a natural but underutilized signal: after each action, the robot directly observes the true next state, revealing the mismatch between predicted and actual outcomes. Building on this insight, we propose feedback world model, a new paradigm that closes the loop between prediction and observation at inference time. Instead of treating the world model as a static open-loop predictor, our method maintains a lightweight feedback state that is updated online to iteratively correct future predictions, compensating for model errors using real-time observations without additional training data or parameter updates. We show that this process can be interpreted as a latent-space observer and admits convergence guarantees under mild conditions. We further introduce action-aware guidance to better translate corrected predictions into control by emphasizing action-controllable components while suppressing irrelevant variations. Experiments on LIBERO-Plus, Robomimic, and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate that our method substantially improves both prediction accuracy and policy performance under distribution shift. In particular, it reduces world model prediction error by up to 76.4% and improves out-of-distribution (OOD) success rate by 30%. These results show that incorporating real-time feedback at inference time provides a simple yet powerful alternative to static world modeling.

</details>


### [117] [Learning Dynamic Pick-and-Place for a Legged Manipulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15713)
*Moonkyu Jung,Jiseong Lee,Zhengmao He,Donghoon Youm,Juhyeok Mun,HyeongJun Kim,Hyunsik Oh,Donghyuk Choi,Jungwoo Hur,Jie Song,Jemin Hwangbo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分层强化学习框架，使四足机器人结合6自由度机械臂能够动态执行抓取-放置任务，在仿真中达到86.05%成功率（负载2.3kg），真实实验中达到73.3%成功率（负载1.3kg，平均执行时间4.06秒），实现了比以往方法更重负载、更大工作空间和更流畅的连续运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腿式机械臂虽然结合了敏捷运动与灵巧操作，但实现协调运动下的精确操控仍是一大难题，现有工作多局限于轻量物体和慢速分段式操作。

Method: 提出分层强化学习框架，包含显式质量估计模块，实现对不同重量物体的自适应全身控制；支持运动与操作并发执行，实现动态连续抓取-放置。

Result: 仿真中负载达2.3kg时成功率86.05%；真实实验中六种场景下负载1.3kg平均成功率73.3%，平均执行时间4.06秒，工作空间从地面到1.1米高桌面。

Conclusion: 该框架展示了四足移动机械臂在较重负载和扩展工作空间下进行自适应全身动态抓取-放置的潜力，优于以往的分段式轻量操作方案。

Abstract: Legged manipulators extend robotic capabilities beyond static manipulation by integrating agile locomotion with versatile arm control. However, achieving precise manipulation while maintaining coordinated locomotion remains a major challenge. This work presents a hierarchical reinforcement learning framework for dynamic pick-and-place tasks using a quadruped equipped with a 6-DOF robotic arm. The framework incorporates an explicit mass estimation module enabling adaptive whole-body control for objects with varying weights. In simulation, the system achieves an 86.05% success rate with payloads up to 2.3 kg. The approach is further validated through real-world experiments across six representative scenarios with controlled variations in object physical properties (size and mass) and task heights. Specifically, within a wide vertical workspace ranging from ground level to 1.1~m-high tabletops, the system demonstrates an average success rate of 73.3% for payloads up to 1.3 kg, with an average execution time of 4.06 s. Unlike prior works that handle lightweight objects and execute pick-and-place motions with slow, piecewise motions, the proposed framework exploits concurrent locomotion and manipulation for dynamic, continuous execution. These results demonstrate the potential of quadrupedal mobile manipulators for adaptive, whole-body pick-and-place with heavier payloads and extended workspaces.

</details>


### [118] [Hierarchical and Holistic Open-Vocabulary Functional 3D Scene Graphs for Indoor Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15753)
*Xinggang Hu,Chenyangguang Zhang,Alexandros Delitzas,Xiangkui Zhang,Marc Pollefeys,Francis Engelmann,Xiangyang Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向桌面环境的开放词汇功能3D场景图构建方法，通过2D视觉定位、跨帧关联和时序图优化，解决了小尺度密集物体在动态视角下的功能关系推理难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有功能3D场景图基准覆盖不足，主要聚焦大型家具，缺少分层结构和桌面级小物体，导致细粒度功能关系推理面临视觉锚定缺失、实例混淆和动态视角归属不确定性等挑战。

Method: 提出基于2D视觉定位和3D图优化的开放词汇流水线：(1) 从2D视觉证据锚定细粒度功能边；(2) 利用多线索关联跨帧节点；(3) 边关联建模为时序图优化，集成证据累积、熵正则化和时序平滑；(4) 全局层次重塑恢复图结构。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法能在真实复杂场景中可靠推断功能3D场景图。

Conclusion: 通过扩展基准覆盖和提出鲁棒的推理流水线，进一步解锁了功能3D场景图在实际应用中的潜力。

Abstract: Functional 3D scene graphs offer a versatile and flexible representation for 3D scene understanding and robotic manipulation, defined by object nodes, interactive elements, and functional relationship edges. However, their potential remains underexplored due to the limited coverage of existing benchmarks and the overly straightforward design of previous pipelines, which primarily focus on large-scale furniture but lack of hierarchical structures. Therefore, in this work, we extend the benchmark coverage by introducing dense tabletop objects and explicit multi-level functional relationships. This expansion introduces critical challenges involving small-scale, dense, and similar instances, with lack of visual anchoring in relational reasoning, instance confusion during cross-frame fusion, and attribution uncertainty under dynamic viewpoints. To address these issues, we propose an open-vocabulary pipeline based on 2D visual grounding and 3D graph optimization. Specifically, we anchor fine-grained functional edges from 2D visual evidence, and associate nodes across frames in 3D using multiple cues. Furthermore, edge association is formulated as temporal graph optimization, integrating evidence accumulation, entropy regularization, and temporal smoothing to robustly determine the functional connections of each node. Finally, global hierarchy shaping is performed to recover the hierarchical graph structure. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed method can reliably infer functional 3D scene graphs in challenging real-world scenes, thereby further unlocking their potential for practical applications.

</details>


### [119] [Lamarckian Inheritance in Dynamic Environments: How Key Variables Affect Evolutionary Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15769)
*K. Ege de Bruin,Kyrre Glette,Kai Olav Ellefsen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究机器人形态与控制的协同优化，发现拉马克式遗传（将学习到的控制器参数传给后代）在动态环境中的优劣取决于两个变量：环境变化与机器人控制的冲突程度，以及这些变化的可预测性。只有当环境变化既冲突又不可预测时，拉马克式遗传才劣于达尔文式遗传。添加传感器可恢复其在冲突环境中的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献对拉马克式遗传在动态环境中的效果存在矛盾结论，作者推测是因为前人未纳入所有相关变量。本文旨在厘清拉马克式遗传在机器人协同优化中真正起作用的边界条件。

Method: 将形态优化视为进化（达尔文式），控制器优化视为终身学习（拉马克式），在虚拟软机器人平台上，使用贝叶斯优化和强化学习两种方法，系统性地操纵环境变化的冲突性和可预测性两个变量，对比拉马克式与达尔文式遗传的表现。

Result: 拉马克式遗传仅在环境变化既与现有控制策略冲突、又对机器人不可预测时，才劣于达尔文式遗传。添加传感器让机器人能感知环境变化，从而预测需要不同行为，可恢复拉马克式遗传的优势。

Conclusion: 拉马克式遗传在机器人进化中的有效性取决于环境动态的两个关键维度（冲突性与可预测性），合理的感知设计可以帮助机器人克服不利条件，实现更优的形态-控制协同优化。

Abstract: The co-optimization of a robot's body and brain presents a coupled challenge: the morphology constrains which control strategies are effective, while the control determines how well the morphology performs. To address this, we combine morphology optimization as evolution with controller optimization as lifetime learning, utilizing Lamarckian inheritance to transfer learned controller parameters from parent to offspring. In dynamic environments, existing literature presents conflicting evidence: while traditional evolutionary theory often suggests Lamarckian inheritance lacks benefit, recent studies in evolutionary robotics indicate it can improve performance. We hypothesize that this is because previous works have not included all relevant variables with dynamic environments. In this work, we show that the benefit of Lamarckian inheritance depends on two variables: how conflicting the environmental changes are to robot control, and the predictability of those changes for the robotic agent. Using virtual soft robots and two different learning approaches, Bayesian optimization and reinforcement learning, we show that Lamarckian inheritance only underperforms Darwinian inheritance when the changes are both conflicting and unpredictable. We find that adding a sensor to detect environmental changes restores the benefits for Lamarckian inheritance in conflicting environments, by allowing robotic agents to predict the need for a different behavior, thereby generalizing their control.

</details>


### [120] [A Topology-Aware Spatiotemporal Handover Framework for Continuous Multi-UAV Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15779)
*Jianlin Ye,Christos Kyrkou,Panayiotis Kolios*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于拓扑时空交接机制的多摄像机多车辆跟踪系统，通过队列匹配算法实现99.8%的交接成功率，显著优于基于外观重识别的基线方法（74.1%），并验证了边缘部署可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机交通监控系统在多摄像机视野中存在轨迹碎片化问题，缺乏跨FOV的车辆身份持续性，无法进行网络级分析如OD估计。基于外观的Re-ID方法在俯视视角下存在视觉模糊性和高计算成本。

Method: 提出轻量级拓扑时空交接机制：利用几何重叠区域和虚拟车道离散化，通过确定性队列匹配算法（FIFO队列）预测性管理身份交接；采用YOLO11+ByteTrack构建高通量并行流水线处理4K视频流。

Result: 在复杂城市环境测试中交接成功率达99.8%，大幅超越Re-ID基线方法的74.1%，验证了边缘部署的可行性。

Conclusion: 基于拓扑的确定性队列匹配方法在实时性、鲁棒性和部署效率上均优于传统外观Re-ID方案，为可扩展的协同交通监控提供了有效路径。

Abstract: The integration of Unmanned Aerial Vehicles(UAVs) into Intelligent Transportation Systems (ITS) offers synoptic visibility for traffic monitoring, yet scalable deployment is hindered by trajectory fragmentation, where vehicle identity persistence is lost across multi-UAV Fields of View (FOV). While state-of-the-art frameworks excel in optimizing local trajectory extraction and stability for single-drone imagery, they often function as isolated data silos that generate disjointed trajectories, thereby precluding network-level analysis such as Origin-Destination estimation. This paper presents a real-time Multi-Camera Multi-Vehicle Tracking (MCMT) system designed to handle global identity persistence. Addressing the visual ambiguity and computational cost of appearance-based Re-Identification (Re-ID) in nadir views, we introduce a lightweight Topology-Based Spatiotemporal Handover mechanism. We implement a high-throughput parallel pipeline leveraging YOLO11 and ByteTrack to process concurrent 4K streams. Our core contribution is a deterministic queue-based matching algorithm that utilizes geometric overlaps and virtual lane discretization to predictively manage identity handover via FIFO queues. Experimental results on complex urban environments, including intersections and merging traffic, demonstrate a Handover Success Rate (HOSR) of 99.8% in continuous traffic flows, significantly outperforming Re-ID baselines (74.1%) while validating edge deployment feasibility. The source code is available at https://github.com/JYe9/multi-camera-multi-vehicle-tracking-system.

</details>


### [121] [Reactive Robot-Centric Safety for Autonomous Navigation in Constrained and Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15782)
*Viswa Narayanan Sankaranarayanan,Vignesh K. Viswanathan,Akshit Saradagi,Sumeet Satpute,George Nikolakopoulos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于3D LIDAR感知和复合控制屏障函数(CBF)的实时安全控制架构，用于自主机器人在空间受限动态环境中的导航。该架构通过机载点云数据动态强制执行避碰约束，并在四足机器人平台上通过地下环境现场实验验证了其在动态障碍物、不安全参考指令、定位异常和狭窄通道等场景下的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决自主机器人在空间受限的动态环境中仅利用机载传感器确保实时安全导航的问题。

Method: 提出一种实时控制架构，集成3D LIDAR感知的复合CBF安全滤波器，将感知驱动的避碰约束直接融入自主管线。安全区域定义为与平台几何一致的本体坐标系椭球，对每个LIDAR点采用时变CBF公式处理世界坐标系下的时变约束。

Result: 在地下环境中使用四足机器人进行视觉检测任务的多次现场实验验证，系统在动态障碍物、不安全高层参考指令、突然定位异常以及穿越狭窄走廊时均表现可靠。

Conclusion: 该感知驱动的CBF安全框架能够在高频控制下处理大量约束，同时对名义任务执行干扰最小，有效提升了机器人在复杂动态环境中的实时安全性。

Abstract: In this work, we address the problem of ensuring real-time safety in autonomous robot navigation, in spatially constrained dynamic environments, by utilizing only onboard sensors. We present a real-time control architecture that integrates a 3D LIDAR perception-based composite control barrier function(CBF)-based safety filter directly into the autonomy pipeline. The proposed perception-driven framework enforces collision avoidance constraints dynamically from onboard point cloud data, thus allowing a large number of constraints to be handled at the control frequency, while remaining minimally invasive to nominal task execution. The safety region is defined as an ellipsoid in the body-frame, consistent with the geometry of the platform, which induces time-varying constraints in the world frame as the robot rotates; this effect is handled through a dedicated formulation of time-varying (CBF) for each LIDAR point. We validate the system through multiple field experiments in underground environments by utilizing a quadruped platform performing a visual inspection task, demonstrating reliable operation in the presence of dynamic obstacles, unsafe high-level references, abrupt localization anomalies, and while traversing through narrow corridors.

</details>


### [122] [GAP: Geometric Anchor Pre-training for Data-Efficient Visuomotor Learning of Manipulation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15836)
*Davide Buoso,Andrea Protopapa,Stefano Di Carlo,Francesca Pistilli,Giuseppe Averta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出几何锚点预训练（GAP），一种轻量级无动作的预热阶段，通过模拟代理任务正则化空间适配器生成稳定的几何锚点，在极少示范（15-50条）和域偏移下显著提升机器人操控策略的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀缺专家示范中学习视觉运动策略时，预训练视觉基础模型（VFM）冻结后，大量任务适应落在小空间池化模块上，该模块容易过拟合到任务无关的捷径，丢失几何基础，且在场景扰动下表现脆弱。需要一种鲁棒性导向的归纳偏置。

Method: GAP在模拟代理任务中预训练池化层，利用免费的对象掩码监督，鼓励适配器生成位于物体上、覆盖其空间范围、且随时间保持尖锐和可重复的关键点，形成稳定的几何锚点。下游模仿学习时保持VFM冻结，仅使用轻量适配器。

Result: 在RoboMimic的Can任务（15示范）上达62%（+16%），Tool Hang任务（50示范）达63%，ManiSkill StackCube（30示范）达61%（+11%），一致超越更强的注意力池化和端到端微调方法。

Conclusion: GAP是一种实用、轻量且与下游任务解耦的预热策略，可跨环境和操控技能复用，为少样本策略学习提供了可靠的几何坐标接口。

Abstract: Learning visuomotor policies from scarce expert demonstrations remains a core challenge in robotic manipulation. A primary hurdle lies in distilling high-dimensional RGB representations into control-relevant geometry without overfitting. While using frozen pre-trained Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) improves data efficiency, it also shifts most task adaptation onto a small spatial pooling module, which can latch onto task-irrelevant shortcuts and lose geometric grounding when finetuned with few data samples. More broadly, pre-trained visual representations used for policy learning have been observed to struggle under even minor scene perturbations, highlighting the need for robustness-oriented inductive biases. We propose Geometric Anchor Pre-training (GAP), a simple, action-free warm-up stage that regularizes the spatial adapter before downstream imitation learning. GAP pre-trains the pooling layer on a lightweight simulated proxy task where object masks are available at no cost, encouraging the adapter to produce keypoints that lie on the object, cover its spatial extent, and remain sharp and repeatable over time. This yields stable geometric anchors that provide a reliable coordinate interface for few-shot policy learning, while keeping the VFM frozen. We evaluate GAP on RoboMimic and ManiSkill under severe data scarcity (15-50 demonstrations) and domain shift. A simple adapter regularized with GAP consistently outperforms stronger attention-based poolers and end-to-end fine-tuning, achieving 62% success on RoboMimic Can with 15 demonstrations (+16% over AFA), 63% on the long-horizon high-precision Tool Hang task with 50 demonstrations, and 61% on ManiSkill StackCube with 30 demonstrations (+11% over full fine-tuning). The proxy stage is lightweight and fully decoupled from downstream tasks, making it practical to reuse across environments and manipulation skills.

</details>


### [123] [Structured Jacobian Construction for Motion Optimization with High-Order Time Derivatives in Multi-Link Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15845)
*Taiki Ishigaki,Ko Ayusawa,Eiichi Yoshida*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向多连杆系统运动优化的结构化雅可比计算框架，能够系统性地推导包含高阶时间导数（如加加速度、力变化率）的运动学和动力学量的解析雅可比表达式，相比数值微分和自动微分方法，在保证精度的同时提升了计算效率，并在逆优化中有效恢复了代价函数权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动优化中涉及高阶时间导数的雅可比计算通常依赖数值微分或自动微分，未能充分利用多连杆系统的结构特性，导致计算成本高且数值不稳定。

Method: 基于综合运动计算框架，提出结构化的雅可比公式化方法，系统推导运动学与动力学量（动量、力、关节力矩等）关于广义坐标及其高阶导数的解析雅可比表达式，适用于正向与逆向优化。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提方法相比数值微分和自动微分显著提升了计算效率，同时保持了相当的精度；在逆优化任务中成功从运动数据中恢复了代价函数权重。

Conclusion: 该框架为涉及高阶时间导数的多连杆系统运动优化提供了一个可扩展、结构化的计算基础。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel framework for Jacobian computation in motion optimization problems involving multi-link systems, where physical quantities are represented using higher-order time derivatives. In motion optimization of robots and humans, cost functions may incorporate higher-order time derivatives, such as jerk or the time variation of forces, to capture smoothness and perceptual characteristics, particularly in motion skill analysis and expressive behaviors, thereby necessitating Jacobian computations involving these quantities. However, such Jacobians are typically computed using numerical or automatic differentiation without explicitly exploiting the underlying multi-link structure, which can lead to increased computational cost and numerical instability. To address this limitation, we propose a structured Jacobian formulation for motion optimization, based on the comprehensive motion computation framework, in which physical quantities and their higher-order time derivatives are systematically represented along the multi-link structure. The proposed method systematically derives analytical expressions for Jacobians of kinematic and dynamic quantities, including momentum, forces, and joint torques, with respect to generalized coordinates and their higher-order derivatives. The resulting framework is applicable to both direct and inverse optimization. Through numerical experiments, we demonstrate that the proposed method improves computational efficiency compared to numerical and automatic differentiation, while achieving comparable accuracy. Furthermore, we demonstrate its effectiveness in inverse optimization by recovering cost function weights from motion data. Together, these results indicate that the proposed formulation provides a scalable and structured computational foundation for motion optimization involving higher-order time derivatives in multi-link systems.

</details>


### [124] [Designing for Robot Wranglers: A Synthesis of Literature and Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15892)
*David Porfirio,Ian McDermott,Hsin-Mei Chen,Satoru Satake,Takayuki Kanda,Thomas D. LaToza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了机器人操作员（robot wrangler）这一新兴角色，通过范围综述和第一手经验反思，揭示了该工作的复杂性和异质性，并提出了支持操作员的设计启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人越来越多地融入人类空间（如医院配送、博物馆互动、仓库管理），需要对负责设置、监控和故障排除的机器人操作员这一利益相关者角色进行深入理解。

Method: 进行了范围综述（scoping review）以梳理文献中的机器人操作活动分类，并结合研究者在各自领域的第一手与想象经验进行反思分析。

Result: 发现"机器人操作"是一个伞式术语，涵盖了高度复杂且异质的活动空间，使得该劳动难以被清晰界定和有效支持。

Conclusion: 提出了支持操作员作为个体以及作为更广泛服务生态系统成员的设计启示。

Abstract: Robots are increasingly present in human spaces, such as for conducting deliveries in hospitals, interacting with visitors at museums, and stocking items in warehouses. To ensure the seamless integration of robots into these spaces, a new role in human-robot interaction is emerging - the robot wrangler, namely an individual who is responsible for setting up, overseeing, and troubleshooting the robot. To understand the needs of this stakeholder, we conducted a scoping review that uncovered a typology of robot wrangling across the research literature, and discovered that wrangling is an umbrella term that collapses a highly complex and heterogeneous space of activities, often rendering this labor difficult to characterize and support. To further clarify and understand robot wrangling, we then reflected on our own firsthand and imagined experiences as robot wranglers within our own respective domains. Guided by the scoping review and our reflections, we devise a series of design implications for supporting wranglers directly as individuals and as members of a wider service ecology.

</details>


### [125] [Dynamic Plasma Shape Control with Arbitrary Sensor Subsets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15935)
*D. Sorokin,M. Stokolesov,A. Granovskiy,I. Prokofyev,E. Adishchev,M. Nurgaliev,E. Khayrutdinov,G. Subbotin,R. Clark,D. Orlov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种强化学习智能体，用于托卡马克等离子体形状控制，能够同时处理动态目标跟踪和传感器故障（30%随机遮蔽），在仿真和DIII-D实验装置上实现了零样本迁移和2.01cm的形状误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统等离子体形状控制依赖平衡重建加线性控制器，要求传感器始终完好，无法应对动态目标。本文旨在用一个强化学习方案同时解决动态形状跟踪和对传感器故障的鲁棒性问题。

Method: 使用NSFsim高保真托卡马克模拟器，基于120个实验形状数据集训练强化学习智能体。采用非对称演员-评论家架构，评论家利用特权平衡信息改善部分可观测下的值估计；演员有辅助形状重构头用于解释性。训练时每0.25秒随机改变形状目标，每回合随机遮蔽30%磁传感器。

Result: 在仿真保持的静态配置上平均形状误差2.01cm；动态轨迹跟踪在仿真和实物上定性验证；策略零样本迁移到物理DIII-D装置和独立GSevolve模拟器。

Conclusion: 强化学习方法能够统一处理动态形状跟踪和传感器故障鲁棒性，无需备份控制器或模式切换逻辑，且可迁移到真实托卡马克装置。

Abstract: Plasma shape control in tokamaks requires a real-time controller that tracks dynamically changing shape targets while tolerating diagnostic failures. Classical approaches decompose the problem into equilibrium reconstruction followed by a linear controller, and assume a fixed, fully operational sensor set. We present a reinforcement learning agent that addresses both limitations simultaneously. The agent is trained in NSFsim, a high-fidelity tokamak simulator configured for DIII-D, on a curated dataset of 120 experimental plasma shapes. The shape targets are resampled as random step changes every 0.25 s, exposing the agent to diverse transitions across the full shape envelope. At test time the agent zero-shot tracks dynamic shape sequences; on a held-out static configuration in simulation it achieves a mean shape error of 2.01 cm, and dynamic trajectory following is demonstrated qualitatively in simulation and on the physical device. Diagnostic dropout randomly masks 30% of magnetic sensors per episode, yielding a single policy robust to arbitrary sensor subsets without backup controllers or mode-switching logic. An asymmetric actor-critic architecture with privileged equilibrium information improves value estimation under partial observability; an auxiliary shape reconstruction head on the actor enables end-to-end shape reconstruction from raw diagnostics and serves as an interpretability tool for policy analysis. The policy transfers to experimental DIII-D shots, where it directly commands the coil actuators on two dynamic shape maneuvers, and to the independent GSevolve simulator.

</details>


### [126] [FocalPolicy: Frequency-Optimized Chunking and Locally Anchored Flow Matching for Coherent Visuomotor Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15944)
*Qian He,Zhenshuo Yang,Wenqi Liang,Chunhui Hao,Nicu Sebe,Jiandong Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FocalPolicy提出了一种结合频率优化分块与局部锚定流匹配的视觉运动策略，通过频域正则化提升跨块动作连贯性，在复杂操作任务中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉运动策略主要优化块内动作分布，忽略了块间连贯性，导致长程动作学习不连贯，无法平衡近端精度与远端预见性。

Method: 提出FocalPolicy，包含频率优化分块（频域正则化改善跨块一致性）和局部锚定流匹配（增强目标信号传播效率），并设计了预见性复合目标函数。

Result: 实验表明FocalPolicy优于现有方法，且各模块具有良好的可迁移性。

Conclusion: 通过频域正则化与局部锚定流匹配，有效实现了近端精度与远端预见性的协同平衡，提升了长程动作的连贯性。

Abstract: Visuomotor policies aim to learn complex manipulation tasks from expert demonstrations. However, generating smooth and coherent trajectories remains challenging, as it requires balancing proximal precision with distal foresight. Existing approaches typically focus on optimizing intra-chunk action distributions, often neglecting the inter-chunk coherence. Consequently, inter-chunk discontinuities significantly impede the learning of coherent long-horizon actions. To overcome this limitation and achieve a synergetic balance between precision and foresight, we propose FocalPolicy, a foresight-aware visuomotor policy that combines Frequency-Optimized Chunking with Locally Anchored flow matching. We introduce a foresight composite objective that supervises time-domain alignment within the proximal actions while regularizing frequency-domain structure over multiple future action chunks to improve cross-chunk coherence. To efficiently learn complex action distributions, we design locally anchored campling to enhance target signal propagation efficiency during consistency flow matching training. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FocalPolicy outperforms existing approaches and confirm the generalizability of our modules to other baselines. Project website: https://focalpolicy.github.io/

</details>


### [127] [A Reproducible and Physically Feasible Dynamic Parameter Identification Framework for a Low-Cost Robot Arm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15949)
*Junji Oaki,Koki Yamane,Koki Inami,Sho Sakaino*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对低成本机器人臂CRANE-X7，提出了一套可复现且物理可行的动态参数识别框架。通过去除惯性积、设计结构化激励轨迹、结合OLS、SDP投影和CLIE优化，最终筛选出统计中心且物理可接受的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本机器人臂（如CRANE-X7）因使用模块化智能驱动器，其动态参数识别面临物理可行性差、参数可辨识性低的问题，亟需一套系统化、可复现的识别流程。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 根据连杆对称性去除惯性积，将刚体模型从65个参数减少到39个；(2) 在关节限位内设计结构化单关节和邻关节激励轨迹；(3) 使用OLS进行逆动力学回归；(4) 通过半定规划（SDP）投影恢复物理可行性；(5) 闭环输入误差（CLIE）精化；(6) 在PCA空间中分析40条轨迹的候选解，选取统计中心模型；(7) 对所有姿态进行惯性矩阵正定性审计，必要时执行局部后CLIE SDP修正。

Result: 实验表明，从OLS到SDP再到CLIE，参数云逐渐集中；最终接受的模型在留出验证运动上保持较高的预测精度，证明了该框架在低成本机器人平台上的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为低成本机器人平台提供了一条通往统计一致且物理可行的动态模型的实际路径，兼顾了可复现性、物理可行性与预测精度。

Abstract: This paper presents a reproducible and physically feasible dynamic parameter identification framework for CRANE-X7, a low-cost robot arm driven by modular smart actuators. To improve practical identifiability, products of inertia are removed according to approximate link symmetry, reducing the rigid-body model from 65 to 39 base parameters. Identification motions are hand-designed from structured single-joint and adjacent-joint primitives under practical joint-range limits. The proposed pipeline combines preprocessing, inverse-dynamics-regressor-based ordinary least squares (OLS), conditional semidefinite-programming (SDP) projection for feasibility recovery, and closed-loop input error (CLIE) refinement. Candidate solutions from 40 structured trajectories are analyzed in a common PCA space to select a statistically central representative model. Because statistical centrality alone does not ensure physical acceptability, the selected model is finally screened by an all-pose positive-definiteness audit of the inertia matrix and, when necessary, corrected by a localized post-CLIE SDP rescue step. Experiments show that the parameter cloud becomes progressively more concentrated from OLS to SDP and CLIE, while the final accepted model preserves high predictive accuracy on held-out validation motions. These results demonstrate a practical route to statistically coherent and physically feasible dynamic models for low-cost robot platforms.

</details>


### [128] [WorldVLN: Autoregressive World Action Model for Aerial Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15964)
*Baining Zhao,Jiacheng Xu,Weicheng Feng,Xin Zhang,Zhaolu Wang,Haoyang Wang,Shilong Ji,Ziyou Wang,Jianjie Fang,Zhiheng Zheng,Weichen Zhang,Yu Shang,Wei Wu,Chen Gao,Xinlei Chen,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出WorldVLN，一种自回归世界动作模型，用于解决航拍视觉-语言导航问题。通过预测潜在世界状态演化并解码为可执行动作，在室内外基准测试中取得12%+成功率提升，并支持零样本迁移到真实无人机部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将航拍VLN视为简单的感知-动作映射问题，缺乏对世界状态演化的前瞻性预测。作者认为应将航拍VLN建模为"预测驱动的世界-动作问题"，让智能体主动想象未来世界状态后再决策行动。

Method: 1) 提出WorldVLN自回归世界动作模型，利用潜在自回归视频骨干网络预测短时域世界状态转换，直接解码为可执行的航点动作；2) 两阶段训练框架：先基于指令条件导航动态进行视频先验接地，再提出Action-aware GRPO强化学习方法，通过下游轨迹后果优化航点决策。

Result: 在公开室外和室内基准测试中，WorldVLN相比现有视觉-语言-动作基线模型获得12%以上的成功率提升，在困难案例上优势更明显；零样本迁移到真实无人机部署取得成功。

Conclusion: WorldVLN通过将航拍VLN重新定义为世界-动作预测问题，结合自回归世界模型与强化学习，显著提升了导航性能。该方法展示了在空间动作任务中利用世界模型进行前瞻性决策的潜力。

Abstract: Aerial vision-language navigation (VLN) requires agents to follow natural-language instructions through closed-loop perception and action in 3D environments. We argue that aerial VLN can be formulated as a prediction-driven world-action problem: the agent should anticipate latent world evolution and act according to the predicted consequences. To this end, we propose WorldVLN, the first autoregressive world action model for aerial VLN. Unlike full-sequence video-generation world models that generate an entire visual clip, WorldVLN adapts a latent autoregressive video backbone to predict short-horizon world-state transitions and directly decodes them into executable waypoint actions. After each action segment is executed, newly received observations are encoded back into the autoregressive context, enabling closed-loop world-action prediction. We further introduce a two-stage training framework that first grounds the video prior in instruction-conditioned navigation dynamics and then develops Action-aware GRPO, the first reinforcement learning method tailored to autoregressive WAMs, to optimize waypoint decisions through their downstream rollout consequences. On public outdoor and indoor benchmarks, WorldVLN consistently outperforms existing Vision-Language-Action baselines with 12\%+ success-rate gains and larger advantages on challenging cases. It further transfers zero-shot to real drone deployment, suggesting that the proposed WorldVLN offers a promising route for spatial action tasks. Demos and code are available at https://embodiedcity.github.io/WorldVLN/.

</details>


### [129] [OHP-RL: Online Human Preference as Guidance in Reinforcement Learning for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15971)
*Yunyang Mo,Jian Li,Qiwei Wu,Yihang Kang,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出OHP-RL框架，将人类干预视为偏好信息而非模仿信号，通过状态依赖的偏好门控机制自适应调节人类反馈对策略学习的影响，在真实机器人操作任务中显著提升效率并降低人类干预成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将人类干预仅作为辅助训练信号，未能充分利用其中蕴含的偏好信息（如安全性、任务约束下的行为偏好），导致探索效率低且不安全。

Method: 提出OHP-RL框架，核心是状态依赖的偏好门控机制，自适应调控人类干预对策略学习的影响时机和程度，在不完美的间歇性人类反馈下仍能保持自主探索和稳定策略优化。

Result: 在三项真实接触式操作任务（Franka机器人）中，OHP-RL在成功率、收敛速度上均优于现有方法，且显著降低了人类干预工作量，学习到的策略表现出更稳定、更符合人类期望的行为。

Conclusion: 将人类干预视为偏好信息而非模仿信号，配合自适应的偏好门控机制，能有效平衡人类引导与自主探索，是提升RL在实际机器人部署中安全性和效率的有效路径。

Abstract: While reinforcement learning (RL) enables robots to acquire skills autonomously, its real-world deployment is severely limited by inefficient and unsafe exploration. Human-in-the-loop interventions offer a practical solution, yet existing methods typically exploit these interventions as auxiliary training signals, without fully capturing the richer information they provide about when and how autonomy should be guided.
  Human interventions often encode relative preferences over behavior under safety and task constraints, rather than prescribing exact actions to imitate. Motivated by this perspective, we propose Online Human Preference as Guidance in Reinforcement Learning (OHP-RL), a framework that leverages human interventions as preference information to guide policy learning. OHP-RL introduces a state-dependent preference gate that adaptively regulates when and to what extent human interventions should shape policy learning. This design enables the agent to benefit from intermittent and imperfect human feedback while preserving autonomous exploration and stable policy optimization.
  We evaluate OHP-RL on three challenging real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks on a Franka robot. Across all tasks, OHP-RL consistently achieves strong success rates, faster convergence, and substantially lower human intervention effort than prior approaches. Moreover, the learned policies exhibit more stable and human-aligned behavior throughout training.

</details>


### [130] [Constrained MPC-Based Motion Planning for Morphing Quadrotors in Ultra-Narrow Passages under Limited Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15999)
*Harsh Modi,Xiao Liang,Minghui Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对可变形四旋翼飞行器在极端受限环境下的形态与轨迹联合规划框架，通过平滑指数障碍代价函数实现基于2D激光雷达的狭窄间隙导航，避免了经典人工势场法在窄通道中的高成本问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典人工势场法在狭窄通道中会产生高成本，人为阻塞可通行路径，导致可变形四旋翼在极限受限环境下（如极窄间隙）难以安全导航。

Method: 提出平滑指数障碍代价函数替代传统人工势场法，引入成本降低因子降低窄通道内的通行代价，无需硬激活阈值；直接利用2D LiDAR测量值构建NMPC框架，嵌入acados求解器实现形态与轨迹联合规划。

Result: 仿真与实验结果表明，该方法能在典型斥力代价函数失败的情况下成功穿越狭窄走廊，计算高效且安全。

Conclusion: 该代价函数具有通用性，不限于可变形四旋翼，适用于任何移动机器人在紧密空间中的安全导航任务。

Abstract: This paper introduces a motion planning framework to plan morphology and trajectory for morphing quadrotors under extremely constrained environments. We develop a novel obstacle avoidance cost function for nonlinear model predictive control (MPC) that enables navigation through extremely narrow gaps under limited perception from a 2D LiDAR. Classical artificial potential field-based costs typically have a high cost in narrow passages, artificially blocking the navigable path. In contrast, we propose a smooth exponential obstacle cost that preserves low traversal cost within narrow gaps while maintaining strong collision avoidance behavior. The formulation avoids hard activation thresholds and introduces a cost reduction factor to reduce the cost within narrow passages. Direct use of 2D LiDAR measurements in MPC allows navigation around arbitrarily shaped obstacles. The method is embedded within an acados-based nonlinear MPC framework. Simulation and experimental results demonstrate successful traversal of narrow corridors where typical repulsive cost functions would fail. The approach provides a computationally efficient and practical solution for navigating through tight spaces while maintaining safety from the obstacles. While we are implementing the framework on the morphing quadrotors, the cost function formulation is general-purpose for any mobile robot application, and is not limited to the morphing quadrotors. The implementation code is available at \href{https://github.com/harshjmodi1996/morphocopter_mpc}{Github Repo} and a short video is available at \href{https://zh.engr.tamu.edu/wp-content/uploads/sites/310/2026/03/MPC_MorphoCopter_video.mp4}{Video Link}.

</details>


### [131] [Fast Expanding Safe Circular Regions for Efficient Local Path Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16009)
*Scott Fredriksson,Akshit Saradagi,George Nikolakopoulos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于几何算法的局部导航方法，通过从激光雷达扫描数据中计算一系列朝向目标方向扩展的圆形区域来捕捉局部可通行空间，相比优化/学习方法计算更快、规划视野更长，并在ROS2中实现验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有局部导航方法（如DWA、MPC、CBF、机器学习等）在简单环境中表现良好，但在复杂场景下基于优化或学习的流程容易遇到困难，需要一种更快速、规划视野更长的方法。

Method: 基于几何算法，从局部LiDAR扫描数据中计算一系列朝向目标方向扩展的圆形区域序列，以此捕捉局部可通行空间，实现局部导航。

Result: 该方法在ROS2框架中实现，并在仿真环境中进行了评估。

Conclusion: 提出了一种计算更快、规划视野更长的几何式局部导航方法，有望在复杂场景中优于传统的优化/学习方法。

Abstract: Local navigation is one of the fundamental problems in robot navigation, and numerous approaches have been proposed over the years, including methods such as the Dynamic Window Approach, Model Predictive Control, and more recently, Control Barrier Functions and machine learning based techniques. While these methods perform well in simple environments, many of them rely on optimization or learning based procedures that can struggle in more complex scenarios. In contrast, this article proposes a more geometric algorithmic approach that enables a local navigation method with faster computation times and longer planning horizons. The proposed method is based on the computation of a sequence of circular regions from a local LiDAR scan that expand in the direction of the goal and capture free local navigable space. The proposed method was implemented in the ROS2 framework and evaluated in a simulated environment.

</details>


### [132] [Adaptive Outer-Loop Control of Quadrotors via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16015)
*Vishnu Saj,Sushi Vemuri,Dileep Kalathil,Moble Benedict*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于残差动力学预测器(RDP)的自适应控制架构，通过主动感知和响应瞬时扰动，在Crazyflie微四旋翼上实现了对外部力/力矩的实时估计，显著优于传统域随机化方法，能够在质量变化、不对称载荷和动态悬挂负载等严重不确定性下保持精确轨迹跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于域随机化(DR)的DRL四旋翼飞行控制策略过于保守，难以应对动态扰动，因此需要一种能够主动感知和响应瞬时扰动的自适应控制方法。

Method: 1) 训练最优的外环策略；2) 用残差动力学预测器(RDP)替代对地面真实扰动数据的依赖，利用历史状态和控制动作在线估计外部力和力矩；3) 引入数据高效的线性校准桥和在线推力修正机制，仅用数秒飞行数据即可将模拟潜空间与真实环境对齐。

Result: 在Crazyflie微四旋翼上的真实世界验证中，所提出的自适应控制器显著优于基线方法，在质量变化、不对称载荷和动态悬挂负载等严重不确定性下仍能保持精确的轨迹跟踪。

Conclusion: 该方法通过主动估计和补偿外部扰动，克服了传统域随机化带来的保守性问题，为四旋翼在复杂动态环境下的鲁棒飞行控制提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) for quadrotor flight control typically relies on Domain Randomization (DR) for sim-to-real transfer, resulting in overly conservative policies that struggle with dynamic disturbances. To overcome this, we propose a novel adaptive control architecture that actively perceives and reacts to instantaneous perturbations. First, we train an optimal outer-loop policy, then replace its reliance on ground-truth disturbance data with a Residual Dynamics Predictor (RDP). The RDP estimates the external forces and moments acting on the aircraft in flight online using only the history of states and control actions. For seamless hardware transfer, we introduce a data-efficient linear calibration bridge and an online thrust correction mechanism that align the simulated latent space with reality using mere seconds of flight data. Real-world validations on a Crazyflie micro-quadrotor demonstrate that our adaptive controller significantly outperforms baselines, maintaining precise trajectory tracking under severe uncertainties including mass variations, asymmetric payloads, and dynamic slung loads

</details>


### [133] [Learning Sim-Grounded Policies for Bimanual Rope Manipulation from Human Teleoperation Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16043)
*Gina Wigginghaus,Tim Missal,Berk Guler,Simon Manschitz,Jan Peters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了在绳索打结任务中，基于状态的策略相比基于视觉的策略能显著提升泛化性能（L1误差降低30.8%），揭示了观察空间本身而非模型架构或数据规模是限制泛化的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形线性物体（如绳索、电缆）在工业和家庭中广泛存在，但其高维状态空间和频繁自遮挡导致操控困难。模仿学习虽有效，但受限于人类遥操作的数据收集成本，因此需要高效利用少量演示数据，选择恰当的观察空间至关重要。

Method: 在相同的双臂遥操作数据集上，比较两种Action Chunking with Transformers（ACT）策略：1）基于视觉的策略，使用两个腕部摄像头采集的RGB图像；2）基于状态的策略，利用多视图融合从初始观测中提取绳索的3D粒子状态，并通过基于粒子的eXtended Position-Based Dynamics仿真进行演化。

Result: 在面对未见过的绳索构型时，基于状态的策略在预测初始抓取-拉动动作的L1误差上比基于视觉的策略降低了30.8%，量化了像素级观测与物理一致性状态之间的可观测性差距。

Conclusion: 状态空间的选择比策略架构或数据规模更关键地影响泛化性能；使用物理一致的状态表示能够更有效地从有限的人类演示中学习可变形线性物体操控任务，指向更数据高效的机器人学习方法。

Abstract: Deformable Linear Objects (DLOs) such as ropes and cables are widely encountered in both household and industrial applications, yet remain challenging to manipulate due to their infinite-dimensional configuration space and frequent self-occlusion. Imitation learning from teleoperation offers a practical path to bimanual DLO manipulation, but its scalability is limited by human effort, making the choice of observation space critical for generalization from small datasets. In this study, we investigate whether the lack of generalization in egocentric visual policies for the knot-untangling task stems from the observation space itself, rather than from the policy architecture or data scale. We compare two Action Chunking with Transformers policies trained on the same bimanual teleoperation data: a vision-based policy conditioned on two egocentric RGB streams from wrist-mounted cameras, and a state-based policy conditioned on the DLO's 3D particle state, extracted from an initial observation via multi-view fusion and evolved in a particle-based eXtended Position-Based Dynamics simulation. Evaluated open-loop on an unseen rope configuration, the state-based policy outperforms its visual counterpart with a 30.8% reduction in L1 error when predicting the initial grasp-and-pull action, quantifying the observability gap between pixels and physics-consistent state, and pointing toward more data-efficient robot learning for the DLO manipulation task from limited human demonstrations.

</details>


### [134] [Health-Conditioned Vision-Language-Action Models for Malfunction-Aware Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16056)
*Hüseyin Arslan,Özgür Erkent*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种健康感知的VLA模型，通过注入轻量级Health Projector模块并输入机器人关节健康状态向量，使机器人能适应关节退化等物理故障，在LIBERO环境中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型虽然能检测和恢复任务失败，但缺乏对机器人物理退化（如关节故障、执行器失效、夹爪无力）的适应能力，而真实场景中机器人常面临此类问题。

Method: 在VLA-Adapter架构中注入Health Projector模块，将机器人关节操作角度和扭矩能力的健康向量作为条件输入，并在LIBERO环境收集的128段遥操作退化数据上训练。

Result: 轻量级增加的模型能成功适应不同配置的退化关节，而默认预训练模型无法完成相同任务。

Conclusion: 健康条件化的VLA方法能有效解决机器人物理故障适应问题，且仅需少量参数增加和有限训练数据即可实现。

Abstract: Research on Vision Language Action (VLA) models has been increasing rapidly in recent years. Although some of them focus on detecting, preventing, and recovering from task failures, they usually don't deal with adapting to robot's physical failures. In real-life scenarios, most robots face physical degradations in various ways such as joint degradation, actuator failure, or weak gripper. We introduce malfunction-aware (health-conditioned) VLA that takes a health vector as an input that gives information about robots' joints' operation angle and torque capability, and adapts its predictions to complete the tasks with the degraded joints. To achieve this, we inject a Health Projector module to the VLA-Adapter architecture and train it on malfunction robot data we collected on the LIBERO environment [1]. We collect 128 teleoperated episodes on Libero-Spatial tasks. Our results show that, with a very lightweight addition, the model can learn to operate successfully with different configurations of degraded joints which the default pretrained VLA-Adapter's Libero-Spatial-Pro model cannot. The code and dataset will be available soon at https://github.com/h-arslan/health-aware-vla

</details>


### [135] [Towards Trustworthy and Explainable AI for Perception Models: From Concept to Prototype Vehicle Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16087)
*Till Beemelmanns,Shayan Sharifi,Manas Mehrotra,Ayushman Choudhuri,Lutz Eckstein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于自动驾驶3D场景理解的可信AI感知模块，该模块同时具备鲁棒性、可解释性（基于注意力的归因图）和校准的不确定性估计，并在实车中部署了可视化XAI界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在自动驾驶感知中占主导地位，但其不透明性违反了可信AI准则，阻碍了安全保证、调试和人类监督。现有的可信AI理论框架缺乏针对3D场景理解的具体实现。

Method: 在Transformer检测器基础上，从注意力机制中提取解释（归因图），并使用基于扰动的测试验证忠实性；集成不确定性估计与校准模块；应用鲁棒性增强训练方法；最终部署到实车并提供XAI界面。

Result: 实验展示了忠实的显著性行为、改进的鲁棒性以及良好校准的不确定性估计，并在实车中实现了实时可信感知监控。

Conclusion: 证明了在自动驾驶3D感知中同时实现鲁棒性、可解释性和不确定性校准是可行的，为可信AI在真实场景中的落地提供了实例。

Abstract: Deep Neural Networks have become the dominant solution for Autonomous Driving perception, but their opacity conflicts with emerging Trustworthy AI guidelines and complicates safety assurance, debugging, and human oversight. While theoretical frameworks for safe and Explainable AI (XAI) exist, concrete implementations of Trustworthy AI for 3D scene understanding remain scarce. We address this gap by proposing a Trustworthy AI perception module that is remarkably robust, integrates faithful explainability, and calibrated uncertainty estimates. Building on a transformer-based detector, we derive explanation from the attention mechanism at inference time and validate their faithfulness using perturbation-based consistency tests. We further integrate an uncertainty estimation and calibration module, and apply robustness-enhancing training methods. Experiments show faithful saliency behavior, improved robustness, and well-calibrated uncertainty estimates. Finally, we deploy these Trustworthy AI elements in a prototype vehicle and provide an XAI Interface that visualizes documentation artifacts, model uncertainty state, and saliency maps, demonstrating the feasibility of trustworthy perception monitoring in real time. Supplementary materials are available at https://tillbeemelmanns.github.io/trustworthy_ai/ .

</details>


### [136] [Beyond Collision Avoidance: Multi-Robot Yielding and Spatial Affordance in Emergency Evacuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16115)
*Ning Zhou,Edmund R. Hunt,Nikolai W. F. Bode*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过虚拟现实游戏实验（N=56），研究了四种多机器人避让策略（Hide、LineEscape、Freeze、ShortestPath）在应急疏散中对人类心理感知的影响。结果表明，主动空间让行策略（Hide）最受青睐，且环境可供性（如避难壁龛）显著影响认知期望，未利用明显环境特征会引发期望违背，导致感知认知延迟大幅增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人在紧急疏散中的让行策略仅关注碰撞避免和宏观流优化，忽略了环境可供性和人类空间期望，缺乏从微观感知层面理解人机交互的机制。

Method: 基于游戏的虚拟疏散实验（N=56），在有无避难壁龛的受限走廊中测试四种机器人让行策略，收集个体心理响应数据。

Result: 偏好层级：Hide > LineEscape > Freeze > ShortestPath；利用壁龛的主动让行增强心理舒适度，未利用明显壁龛引发期望违背，显著增加感知认知延迟；先前的机器人交互经验有助于用户理解复杂社交意图。

Conclusion: 紧急情况下的人机安全交互需从纯轨迹优化转向语义感知导航，未来将扩展至机器人群体与行人人群的复杂交互研究。

Abstract: As mobile service robots increasingly coexist with pedestrians, ensuring passively safe behaviour during confined emergency evacuations is critical. Existing multi-robot yielding strategies often focus solely on collision avoidance and macroscopic flow optimisation, overlooking environmental affordances and human spatial expectations. To bridge the gap between macroscopic theory and micro-level perception, we conducted a game-based virtual evacuation experiment (N=56). We investigated individual psychological responses to four multi-robot yielding strategies (Hide, LineEscape, Freeze, ShortestPath) across confined corridors with and without refuge niches. Our results establish a robust preference hierarchy (Hide > LineEscape > Freeze > ShortestPath), demonstrating that proactive space-yielding significantly outperforms freezing and efficiency-first approaches. Crucially, we found that environmental affordances heavily shape cognitive expectations. Actively utilising available niches amplifies the psychological comfort of proactive yielding (Hide). Conversely, failing to use an obvious niche (e.g., executing LineEscape) may trigger Expectation Violation. This is reflected in a drastically increased perceived cognitive delay, despite objectively unimpeded trajectories. Furthermore, prior robot interaction experience helps users decode complex social intents. Ultimately, this research demonstrates that safe human-robot interaction during emergencies must evolve from pure trajectory optimisation to semantically aware navigation. Future work will extend this framework to investigate complex interactions between robot swarms and pedestrian crowds.

</details>


### [137] [DexJoCo: A Benchmark and Toolkit for Task-Oriented Dexterous Manipulation on MuJoCo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16257)
*Hanwen Wang,Weizhi Zhao,Xiangyu Wang,Siyuan Huang,He Lin,Boyuan Zheng,Rongtao Xu,Gang Wang,Yao Mu,He Wang,Lue Fan,Hongsheng Li,Zhaoxiang Zhang,Tieniu Tan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexJoCo是一个面向灵巧手操作任务的基准测试与工具包，包含11个功能导向任务、1.1K条轨迹数据，支持领域随机化与多维度模型评估，揭示了当前灵巧操作策略的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧手基准缺乏能体现灵巧手相较于平行夹爪独特操作能力的任务，且缺少全面的评估流程，因此需要建立更标准化的评估体系。

Method: 构建了11个功能导向的任务（涵盖工具使用、双手协调、长时程执行与推理），开发低成本数据采集系统收集1.1K条轨迹，支持领域随机化，并在视觉/动力学随机化、多任务训练、动作头适配等设置下对现代模型进行基准测试。

Result: 通过大量实证分析，识别出当前灵巧操作策略的重要洞见与普遍局限性。

Conclusion: DexJoCo揭示了当前灵巧手学习策略的共同短板，为未来灵巧手机器人学习研究指明了关键挑战。

Abstract: Achieving human-level manipulation requires dexterous robotic hands capable of complex object interactions. Advancing such capabilities further demands standardized benchmarks for systematic evaluation. However, existing dexterous benchmarks lack tasks that reflect the unique manipulation capabilities of dexterous hands over parallel grippers, as well as comprehensive evaluation pipelines. In this paper, we present DexJoCo, a benchmark and toolkit for task-oriented dexterous manipulation, comprising 11 functionally grounded tasks that evaluate tool-use, bimanual coordination, long-horizon execution, and reasoning. We develop a low-cost data collection system and collect 1.1K trajectories across these tasks, with support for domain randomization to assess robustness. We benchmark modern models under diverse settings, including visual and dynamics randomization, multi-task training, and action-head adaptation. Through extensive empirical analysis, we identify several important insights and common limitations of current policies in dexterous manipulation, highlighting key challenges for future research in dexterous hand robot learning. Project page available at: https://dexjoco.github.io

</details>


### [138] [DualReg: Dual-Space Filtering and Reinforcement for Rigid Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.17034)
*Jiayi Li,Yuxin Yao,Qiuhang Lu,Juyong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DualReg双空间配准范式，结合特征匹配（处理大变换）与局部几何匹配（精细对齐），通过轻量级RANSAC过滤+几何代理优化，在KITTI上实现比MAC快32倍且精度相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刚性配准面临噪声、部分重叠和实时性挑战。特征匹配鲁棒但精度有限，局部几何匹配精度高但依赖良好初值。现有方法无法同时兼顾两者的优势。

Method: 1) 提出双空间范式，先进行特征匹配；2) 设计轻量级单点RANSAC过滤+精炼模块去除不可靠匹配；3) 将过滤后的匹配视为锚点，提取几何代理；4) 构造含定制求解器的目标函数估计变换。

Result: 在KITTI数据集上，与MAC相比实现了32倍的CPU时间加速，同时保持相当的配准精度。

Conclusion: 提出的双空间范式有效融合了特征匹配与局部几何匹配的优点，在保持精度的同时大幅提升计算效率，适合实时应用场景。

Abstract: Noisy, partially overlapping data and the need for real-time processing pose major challenges for rigid registration. Considering that feature-based matching can handle large transformation differences but suffers from limited accuracy, while local geometry-based matching can achieve fine-grained local alignment but relies heavily on a good initial transformation, we propose a novel dual-space paradigm to fully leverage the strengths of both approaches. First, we introduce an efficient filtering mechanism consisting of a computationally lightweight one-point RANSAC algorithm and a subsequent refinement module to eliminate unreliable feature-based correspondences. Subsequently, we treat the filtered correspondences as anchor points, extract geometric proxies, and formulate an effective objective function with a tailored solver to estimate the transformation. Experiments verify our method's effectiveness, as demonstrated by a 32x CPU-time speedup over MAC on KITTI with comparable accuracy. Project page: https://ustc3dv.github.io/DualReg/.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [139] [DeepSlide: From Artifacts to Presentation Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15202)
*Ming Yang,Zhiwei Zhang,Jiahang Li,Haoseng Liu,Yuzheng Cai,Weiguo Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeepSlide是一个人工参与的多智能体系统，专注于优化演示文稿的交付过程（节奏、叙事、排练），而不仅仅是生成视觉上合理的幻灯片。它通过逻辑链规划器、内容树检索、马尔可夫渲染和沙盒执行等模块，在保持幻灯片质量的同时，显著提升了叙事流畅性、节奏精度和讲稿-幻灯片协同效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI幻灯片生成器主要优化"制品"（视觉上合理的幻灯片），而忽视了"交付过程"（节奏、叙事和演讲准备），导致生成的幻灯片在演讲时效果不佳。

Method: 提出DeepSlide多智能体系统，包含：(i) 可控逻辑链规划器（带每节点时间预算），(ii) 轻量级内容树检索器（用于知识溯源），(iii) 马尔可夫式序列渲染（带样式继承），(iv) 沙盒执行与最小修复。同时引入双记分板评估基准，区分静态制品质量与动态交付质量。

Result: 在20个领域和多种受众场景中，DeepSlide在制品质量上与强基线持平，在交付指标上持续取得更大增益，显著改善了叙事流、节奏精度、幻灯片-讲稿协同及注意力引导。

Conclusion: 优化演示交付过程（而非仅视觉制品）对于学术演讲AI系统更为关键，DeepSlide证明了人机协作的多智能体架构在提升整体演讲效果方面的有效性。

Abstract: Presentations are a primary medium for scholarly communication, yet most AI slide generators optimize the artifact (a visually plausible deck) while under-optimizing the delivery process (pacing, narrative, and presentation preparation). We present DeepSlide, a human-in-the-loop multi-agent system that supports preparing the full presentation process, from requirement elicitation and time-budgeted narrative planning, to evidence-grounded slide--script generation, attention augmentation, and rehearsal support. DeepSlide integrates (i) a controllable logical-chain planner with per-node time budgets, (ii) a lightweight content-tree retriever for grounding, (iii) Markov-style sequential rendering with style inheritance, and (iv) sandboxed execution with minimal repair to ensure renderability. We further introduce a dual-scoreboard benchmark that cleanly separates static artifact quality from dynamic delivery excellence. Across 20 domains and diverse audience profiles, DeepSlide matches strong baselines on artifact quality while consistently achieving larger gains on delivery metrics, improving narrative flow, pacing precision, and slide--script synergy with clearer attention guidance.

</details>


### [140] [SDOF: Taming the Alignment Tax in Multi-Agent Orchestration with State-Constrained Dispatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15204)
*Zhantao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SDOF是一个将多智能体执行建模为约束状态机（FSM）的框架，通过在线RLHF意图路由器、状态感知调度器和技能注册表三层防御，在招聘系统基准上实现86.5%任务完成率，并100%拦截注入攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LangChain等编排框架仅做图路由，未强制执行业务流程的阶段约束，导致缺乏可审计的执行控制和安全保障。

Method: 将多智能体执行视为受约束的有限状态机，包含：(1) GRPO训练的在线RLHF专用意图路由器；(2) 状态感知调度器（GoalStage有限自动机检查）；(3) 技能注册表（前置/后置条件验证）。

Result: 在6000+企业招聘系统上：GSPO对齐的7B意图路由器联合准确率80.9%（vs GPT-4o的48.9%）；端到端任务完成率86.5%；注入攻击拦截率100%；消息级审计精确率100%、召回率88%。

Conclusion: SDOF通过状态机约束与分层防御，显著提升了多智能体系统的业务合规性、可审计性和安全性，为工业级Agent编排提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent orchestration frameworks such as LangChain, LangGraph, and CrewAI route tasks through graph-based pipelines but do not enforce the stage constraints that govern real business processes. We present SDOF, a framework that treats multi-agent execution as a constrained state machine. SDOF operates through two primary defensive layers, implemented by three components: (1) an Online-RLHF Specialized Intent Router trained via Generative Reward Modeling (GRPO) and (2) a StateAwareDispatcher with GoalStage finite-automaton checks and precondition/postcondition SkillRegistry validation for auditable execution control. On a recruitment system backed by the Beisen iTalent platform (6000+ enterprises), 185 expert-curated scenarios trigger 1671 live API calls. Our GSPO-aligned 7B Intent Router achieves higher joint accuracy than zero-shot GPT-4o on this FSM-constrained adversarial routing benchmark (80.9% versus 48.9%). In end-to-end execution, SDOF reaches 86.5% task completion (95% confidence interval 80.8 to 90.7) and blocks all 22 operations in the injection, illegal HR subset. Under a broader message-level blocking audit, SDOF attains precision 100% and recall 88%, expert agreement kappa=0.94. A separate evaluation on 960 SGD-derived dialogues spanning 8 service domains surfaces 201 stage-order conflicts under our FSM mapping, 41 of which arise in the normal split. This arXiv version reports the current validated scope; extended multi-seed training comparisons and deeper workflow evaluations will be released in a subsequent update.

</details>


### [141] [Does Theory of Mind Improvement Really Benefit Human-AI Interactions? Empirical Findings from Interactive Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15205)
*Nanxu Gong,Zixin Chen,Haotian Li,Zishu Zhao,Jianxun Lian,Huamin Qu,Yanjie Fu,Xing Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出交互式ToM评估新范式，发现静态基准测试的提升并不总能转化为动态人机交互中的性能改善，强调基于交互的评估对开发社交感知LLM的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ToM能力评估多采用第三人称故事阅读和选择题形式，忽略了人机交互中第一人称、动态和开放性的特点，无法直接衡量ToM提升技术对实际交互的帮助。

Method: 提出交互式ToM评估新范式，包含视角转换和度量转换；对四种代表性ToM增强技术进行系统研究，使用四个真实数据集和用户研究，覆盖目标导向任务（编程、数学）和经验导向任务（咨询）。

Result: 静态基准测试上的提升并不总能转化为动态人机交互中的更好表现。

Conclusion: 基于交互的评估对于开发下一代具有社交感知能力的LLM以达成人机共生至关重要。

Abstract: Improving the Theory of Mind (ToM) capability of Large Language Models (LLMs) is crucial for effective social interactions between these AI models and humans. However, the existing benchmarks often measure ToM capability improvement through story-reading, multiple-choice questions from a third-person perspective, while ignoring the first-person, dynamic, and open-ended nature of human-AI (HAI) interactions. To directly examine how ToM improvement techniques benefit HAI interactions, we first proposed the new paradigm of interactive ToM evaluation with both perspective and metric shifts. Next, following the paradigm, we conducted a systematic study of four representative ToM enhancement techniques using both four real-world datasets and a user study, covering both goal-oriented tasks (e.g., coding, math) and experience-oriented tasks (e.g., counseling). Our findings reveal that improvements on static benchmarks do not always translate to better performance in dynamic HAI interactions. This paper offers critical insights into ToM evaluation, showing the necessity of interaction-based assessments in developing next-generation, socially aware LLMs for HAI symbiosis.

</details>


### [142] [SkillSmith: Compiling Agent Skills into Boundary-Guided Runtime Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15215)
*Duling Xu,Zheng Chen,Zaifeng Pan,Jiawei Guan,Dong Dong,Jialin Li,Bangzheng Pu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillSmith 提出了一种边界优先的编译器-运行时框架，通过将技能包离线编译为最小可执行接口，减少了 LLM Agent 系统中不必要的上下文注入和重复推理开销，在 SkillsBench 上实现了解答阶段 token 用量减少 57.44%、推理迭代减少 42.99%、时间加速 2.02 倍等显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM Agent 系统中，技能通常作为上下文指导直接注入推理循环，这带来了两大冗余问题：不相关上下文的注入，以及重复的技能推理与规划。作者旨在解决这些效率瓶颈。

Method: 提出 SkillSmith，一种"边界优先"的编译器-运行时框架。离线阶段将技能包编译为最小可执行接口，提取细粒度的操作边界；运行时 Agent 仅动态访问和执行相关组件，避免不必要的上下文注入和推理开销。

Result: 在 SkillsBench 基准上，相比使用原始技能，SkillSmith 实现了解答阶段 token 用量减少 57.44%，推理迭代减少 42.99%，解答时间减少 50.57%（加速 2.02 倍），货币成本降低 57.44%。并且更强模型编译的产物可被更小模型复用，提升任务准确率。

Conclusion: SkillSmith 通过边界优先的编译范式有效解决了技能注入带来的冗余问题，显著提升效率，同时具有良好的可迁移性——强模型的编译产物可赋能弱模型。

Abstract: Recently, skills have been widely adopted in large language model (LLM)-based agent systems across various domains. In existing frameworks, skills are typically injected into the agent reasoning loop as contextual guidance once matched to a runtime task, enabling specialized task-solving capabilities. We find that this execution paradigm introduces two major sources of redundancy: irrelevant context injection and repeated skill-specific reasoning and planning. To this end, we propose SkillSmith, a boundary-first compiler-runtime framework that compiles skill packages offline into minimal executable interfaces. By extracting fine-grained operational boundaries from skills, SkillSmith enables agents to dynamically access and execute only the relevant components at runtime, thereby minimizing unnecessary context injection and redundant reasoning overhead. In the evaluation on SkillsBench benchmark, SkillSmith reduces solve-stage token usage by 57.44%, thinking iterations by 42.99%, solve time by 50.57% (2.02x faster), and token-proportional monetary cost by 57.44% compared with using raw-skills. Moreover, compiled artifacts produced by a stronger model can be reused by a smaller or more efficient runtime model, improving task accuracy in cases where raw skill interpretation fails. The source code and data are available at https://github.com/AetherHeart-AI/Aeloon.

</details>


### [143] [Fair outputs, Biased Internals: Causal Potency and Asymmetry of Latent Bias in LLMs for High-Stakes Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15217)
*Jagdish Tripathy,Marcus Buckmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 指令微调的语言模型在高风险决策中表现出行为公平性，但内部表示仍保留偏见关联。这些被压抑的偏见表示不仅具有决策相关性，而且在不同人口群体间表现出不对称性，可以通过激活干预、对抗性提示和参数高效微调被重新激活，表明仅关注输出的行为审计不足以确保AI治理安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示指令微调模型中被压制的内部偏见表示是否仍具有因果决策影响力，以及这种影响力在不同人口群体间是否对称，从而指出输出级行为审计的局限性。

Method: 使用匹配的抵押贷款申请（仅种族相关名称不同）测试开放权重模型；通过激活引导和跨层干预技术，分析模型内部表示对决策的因果影响。

Result: 模型在输出层无偏见，但内部层保留了并放大了人口统计信息；重新注入这些信息可导致近乎完全的决策反转；这种潜在偏见在人口群体间具有不对称性，且易受对抗性提示和参数高效微调攻击。

Conclusion: 行为审计（仅关注输出）不足以检测内部偏见风险，建议采用结合输出评估与表示分析的"双层测试框架"来治理高风险决策中的AI系统。

Abstract: Instruction-tuned language models exhibit behavioural fairness in high-stakes decisions while retaining biased associations in their internal representations. However, whether these suppressed representations can affect model outputs - and whether such causal potency is symmetric across demographic groups - remains unknown. We investigate the use of open-weight models for mortgage underwriting using matched applications that differ only in racially-associated names and reveal a critical disconnect: models show no output-level bias, yet retain and amplify demographic representations across model layers. Through activation steering and novel cross-layer interventions, we demonstrate that this suppressed information is decision-relevant: when reinjected at critical layers, it produces near-complete decision reversals. Critically, this latent bias is asymmetric - steering interventions affect decisions in one demographic direction, while producing minimal effects in reverse - and susceptible to adversarial prompt engineering and parameter-efficient fine-tuning. These findings demonstrate that behavioural audits focused on outputs are insufficient: fair outputs can mask exploitable internal biases. They also motivate dual-layer testing frameworks combining output evaluation with representational analysis for AI governance in high-stakes decisions.

</details>


### [144] [CAX-Agent: A Lightweight Agent Harness for Reliable APDL Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15218)
*Chenying Lin,Yichen Hai,Yi He,Ran Wang,Haiyan Qiang,Liang Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CAX-Agent轻量级Agent Harness架构，用于解决LLM在MAPDL有限元仿真中的可靠性问题。通过三层架构（LLM服务层、Agent管理层、求解器后端）和恢复策略阶梯，实验表明基于模型的恢复策略（model_only）在任务完成率（0.93）、评分（3.59/4）和零干预率（0.84）上显著优于纯规则恢复和无恢复策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型直接用于MAPDL有限元仿真时存在输出不一致、任务失败频繁等可靠性问题，缺乏结构化执行控制、工具封装和故障恢复机制。

Method: 提出CAX-Agent三层架构：LLM服务层、Agent管理层（核心为恢复阶梯）和求解器后端。恢复阶梯从确定性规则修补、模型驱动再生、上下文丰富到人工干预逐级升级。实验对比三种恢复策略（无恢复、仅规则、仅模型），在50个标准结构基准上重复3次（共450个案例），由两名独立评分者盲评。

Result: 模型驱动恢复策略（model_only）表现最优：任务完成率0.9267，任务评分3.59/4，总分9.16/10，零干预率0.84。仅规则恢复（rule_only）为0.7733/3.17/7.03/0.00，无恢复（no_recovery）为0.6933/2.74/5.60/0.00。效应量大（Cliff's delta=0.81-0.87），评分者间信度高（Cohen's kappa=0.84）。

Conclusion: 模型驱动的恢复策略在MAPDL仿真任务中显著优于规则驱动和无恢复策略，但基准测试采用简单几何体以隔离恢复策略影响，结论的泛化性需在更复杂场景中进一步验证。

Abstract: Large language models deployed for MAPDL finite-element simulation face practical reliability challenges: without structured execution control, tool encapsulation, and fault recovery, outputs may be inconsistent and task failures are common. The Agent Harness paradigm addresses this by inserting domain-specific orchestration middleware that manages tool lifecycles, workflow state, and recovery escalation. This paper presents the architecture of CAX-Agent, a lightweight agent harness purpose-built for MAPDL automation, and empirically evaluates one of its core components -- the recovery policy.CAX-Agent organizes execution into three layers -- LLM service, agent harness, and solver backend -- with a recovery ladder that escalates from deterministic rule patching through model-driven regeneration to context enrichment and human intervention. We evaluate three recovery strategies (no_recovery, rule_only, and model_only) on 50 standard structural benchmarks with three repeated runs per strategy (450 case-runs total). Two independent human raters score task completion under blind conditions; inter-rater agreement is strong (quadratic weighted Cohen's kappa = 0.84, 96 percent of score pairs within one point). Model_only achieves the best completion rate (0.9267), task score (3.59/4), total score (9.16/10), and zero-intervention rate (0.84), outperforming rule_only (0.7733, 3.17/4, 7.03/10, 0.00) and no_recovery (0.6933, 2.74/4, 5.60/10, 0.00) with large effect sizes (Cliff's delta = 0.81-0.87). The benchmark uses deliberately simple geometries to isolate recovery-policy effects; we discuss the scope of these findings and directions for broader validation.

</details>


### [145] [NOVA: Fundamental Limits of Knowledge Discovery Through AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15219)
*Salman Avestimehr,Ken Duffy,Muriel Médard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NOVA框架，将AI自我改进的“生成-验证-积累-重训”循环建模为知识空间的自适应采样过程。研究发现当简单知识被耗尽后，即使很小的误报率也会导致无效知识污染知识库；并证明在尾等价假设下，获得D个不同真知灼见的累积生成成本满足R_cum(D)=Θ(c_gen D^α)，呈现收益递减的幂律缩放规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI系统能否通过迭代自我改进发现真正的新知识，以及实现这种发现的成本效益关系，特别是在长期发现过程中面临的理论极限和失败模式。

Method: 将“生成-验证-积累-重训”循环建模为知识空间上的自适应采样过程，通过数学分析识别知识覆盖有限域的条件及其违反导致的四种失败模式，并结合Good-Turing估计和尾等价假设推导发现成本的幂律缩放规律。

Result: 识别出污染、遗忘、探索失败和接受失败四种模式；发现“污染陷阱”：易发现知识耗尽后，模型对有效新知识分配的质量缩小，即使很小误报率也会导致无效知识更快积累；证明在Zipf尾等价假设下，发现成本满足R_cum(D)=Θ(c_gen D^α)的幂律缩放关系。

Conclusion: AI系统自我改进发现新知识存在根本性的成本瓶颈和污染风险，专家输入在接近自主探索边界时最有价值，为理解AI知识发现的理论极限提供了量化分析框架。

Abstract: Can AI systems discover genuinely new knowledge through iterative self improvement, and if so, at what cost? We introduce the NOVA framework, which models the common ``generate, verify, accumulate, retrain'' loop as an adaptive sampling process over a knowledge space. We identify sufficient conditions under which accumulated genuine knowledge eventually covers a finite domain, and show how their violations produce distinct failure modes: contamination, forgetting, exploration failure, and acceptance failure. We then analyze imperfect verification and identify a contamination trap: as easy-to-find knowledge is exhausted, the model mass assigned to new valid artifacts shrinks, so even small false-positive rates can cause invalid artifacts to enter the knowledge base faster than genuine discoveries. We clarify that Good--Turing estimation is a local batch-diversity diagnostic, not an estimator of the historically undiscovered valid mass that governs long-term discovery. Under a separate tail-equivalence assumption relating the model's effective discovery distribution to a Zipf law with exponent $α>1$, we prove that the cumulative generation cost required to obtain $D$ distinct genuine discoveries satisfies $R_{\mathrm{cum}}(D)=Θ(c_{\mathrm{gen}}D^α)$, where $c_{\mathrm{gen}}$ is the per-candidate generation cost. This scaling law quantifies asymptotic diminishing returns as the discovery frontier advances. Finally, we formalize human amplification through guidance, generation, and verification, explaining why expert input is most valuable near autonomous exploration barriers.

</details>


### [146] [ICRL: Learning to Internalize Self-Critique with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15224)
*Jianbo Lin,Xiaomin Yu,Yi Xin,Yifu Guo,Zhuosong Jiang,Zhongqi Yue,Weishi Wang,Heqing Zou,Chengwei Qin,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ICRL框架，通过强化学习联合训练求解器和批判者，让模型内化自我批判能力，在没有外部批判的情况下也能自我改进。在Agentic和数学推理任务上相比GRPO平均提升6.4和7.0个点，8B批判者可达32B水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型智能体虽然可以通过外部批判纠正错误，但一旦移除批判，模型又会回到错误状态，批判没有被内化为能力。同时，冻结的批判者无法持续提升反馈质量，限制了模型的自我迭代改进潜力。

Method: 提出ICRL框架：（1）从共享骨干网络联合训练求解器和批判者；（2）批判者基于求解者后续性能提升获得奖励，激励生成可操作的反馈；（3）引入分布校准重加权比（distribution-calibration re-weighting ratio），选择性迁移与求解器自身提示分布兼容的批判引导改进；（4）角色分组优势估计（role-wise group advantage estimation）稳定两个角色的联合优化。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-8B上进行评估，在Agentic任务上比GRPO平均提升6.4个点，数学推理任务上提升7.0个点。学习得到的8B批判者与32B批判者性能相当，且使用更少的token。

Conclusion: ICRL成功让模型内化自我批判能力，使求解器无需外部批判即可自我改进，批判者也通过强化学习持续提升反馈质量，实现了有效的自我迭代改进。

Abstract: Large language model-based agents make mistakes, yet critique can often guide the same model toward correct behavior. However, when critique is removed, the model may fail again on the same query, indicating that it has not internalized the critique's guidance into its underlying capability. Meanwhile, a frozen critic cannot improve its feedback quality over time, limiting the potential for iterative self-improvement. To address this, we propose learning to internalize self-critique with reinforcement learning(ICRL), a novel framework that jointly trains a solver and a critic from a shared backbone to convert critique-induced success into unassisted solver ability. The critic is rewarded based on the solver's subsequent performance gain, incentivizing actionable feedback.
